  515 | ): Promise<Response> {
  516 |   const outcome = await call;
  517 | 
  518 |   if (outcome === null) {
  519 |     // The app refused before sending: no credits shows a notice and stops here.
  520 |     throw new Error(`${what} sent no request${await whatTheAppSaid(page)}.${alsoSeen(page)}`);

  531 |   // waitForResponse observes headers. The core API client decodes the body before
  532 |   // resolving api.post<string>(), so wait for that body before checking UI state.

  537 |     () => outcome.response.finished(),

  549 |       .catch(() => '');

  558 | async function whatTheAppSaid(page: Page): Promise<string> {
  559 |   // Next.js's route announcer is also role="alert" and holds the page title; the
  560 |   // app's error notification is the alert carrying antd's close-circle icon.
  561 |   const notice = lowCredits(page)
  562 |     .notice.or(
  563 |       page.getByRole('alert').filter({ has: page.getByRole('img', { name: 'close-circle' }) })
  564 |     )
  565 |     .first();
  566 | 
  567 |   const said = await notice
  568 |     .innerText()
  569 |     .then((text) => text.trim().replaceAll(/\s+/g, ' '))
  570 |     .catch(() => '');
  571 | 
  572 |   return said === '' ? '' : `, and said "${said}"`;
  573 | }
  574 | 
  575 | function alsoSeen(page: Page): string {
  576 |   const problems = pageProblems(page);
  577 |   return problems.length === 0 ? '' : `\nThe page also saw:\n${problems.join('\n')}`;
  578 | }

  580 | /**
  581 |  * Moves to one of the editor's tabs.
  582 |  *
  583 |  * The pointer is left wherever the last click landed, and a tooltip that opens
  584 |  * under it is drawn into a popper that can cover the tab bar and swallow every
  585 |  * click until the action gives up — thirty seconds spent being told that
  586 |  * "virtual" intercepts pointer events. Escape closes whatever is showing, and
  587 |  * what it dismissed stays shut until the pointer leaves and comes back, which
  588 |  * the click itself does.
  589 |  */
  590 | async function openTab(
  591 |   page: Page,
  592 |   editor: ReturnType<typeof scanConfigEditor>,
  593 |   id: string
  594 | ): Promise<void> {
  595 |   await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
  596 |   await editor.tab(id).click(NO_NAVIGATION);
  597 | }
  598 | 
  599 | /**
  600 |  * Waits for a coordinate to stop moving.
  601 |  *
  602 |  * The wait reads whatever the page shows; the editor asks the server itself.
  603 |  * A reload mid-wait sends the page back to the campaign (see `keepCampaign`),
  604 |  * and the locator then reads the reopened page. A run longer than the `@slow`
  605 |  * job can hold has to be picked up from the Workflows activity table instead
  606 |  * of followed from here.
  607 |  *
  608 |  * `toPass` rather than a single long `toHaveText`, so a run that ends in
  609 |  * "error" is reported as that instead of as a timeout with nothing to say.
  610 |  */
  611 | export async function waitForCampaign(status: Locator, minutes: number): Promise<void> {
  612 |   await expect(async () => {
  613 |     const text = (await status.innerText()).trim();
  614 |     expect(text, `The campaign is still "${text}".`).toMatch(SETTLED);
> 615 |   }).toPass({ timeout: minutes * 60_000, intervals: [POLL_INTERVAL] });
      |     ^ Error: The campaign is still "Running".
  616 | }
